8a4(gzungs): to'i bu 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pas shes rab dang ldan pa rnams kyi mchog tu bstan pa lags na bstan pa 'di gang la bstan pa lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka'-i stsal ba/'jam dpal ngas gang bstan pa 'di ni bstan pa med pa'o//de nas 'jam dpal gzhon nur
8a5(gzungs): gyur pas gnas brtan sha ra dwa ti'i bu la 'di skad ces smras so//gnas brtan gyis nyan thos kyi chos rnams thob bam/smras pa de ni ma yin no//'jam dpal gyis smras pa/'o na ci khyod so so'i skye bo'am/smras pa de ni ma yin na'o//'jam dpal gyis smras ba/
8a6(gzungs): 'o na ci khyod slob pa'am/smras pa/de ni ma yin no//'jam pa dpal gyis smras pa/'o na/khyod ci shes rab dang ldan pa'i mchog yin nam/smras pa/de ni ma yin//'jam dpal gyis smras la/gal te btsun pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu so so'i skye bo 
8a7(gzungs): sang ma yin/slob pa yang ma yin/shes rab dang ldan pa'i mchod kyang ma yin na de nyid khyod dmigs pa can du 'gyur ro//sha ra dwa ti'i bus smras pa/rigs kyi bu kho bo ni skyes bu dam pa rgya mtsho ltar gting dpag dka' ba khyod dang 'gyed par mi byed do//'jam 
8b1(gzungs): dpal gyis smras pa/btsun pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu khyod ni rgan rabs thu bo mchog yin gyis btsun pa sha ra dwa ti'i bude skad ma zer cig/gnas brtan gyis smras pa/rigs kyi bu rgas pa'i dngos pos ni ci yang 'thob par mi 'gyur/mngon par rtogs par mi 'gyur ro//
8b2(gzungs): rigs kyi bu don de nyid shes par bya ba'i phyir khyod la dpe bya'o//rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na rdo rje ngan ngon gyis kyang ri brag chen po rnam par 'jig go//rigs kyi bu de bzhin du khyod kyi spu'i khung bu gcig na shes rab 'dug pa gang yin pa de ni kho bo lta bu'i 
8b3(gzungs): sems can rdul phra rab kyi rdul snyed rnams la med do//rigs kyi bu don de nyid she sa par bya ba'i phyir yang khyod la dpe bya'o//rig kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na/ba lang blang po che shin tu mi bzad pa chen po de yang mi stobs dang ldan pas lcags kyu chung dus dul bar 
8b4(gzungs): byed do//rigs kyi bu de bzhin tu khyod ni stobs dang ldan pa/kho bo ni nyam chung ba ste/slang po che chen po 'di lta bu dang kho bo 'gyed ga la nus/de nas bcom ldan 'das la gnas brtan sha ra dwa ti'i bus 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das 'di lta ste/dper 
8b5(gzungs): na mi dmus long gis shul tu mchi ba'am/grong khyer mthong bar yang rngo mi thogs na phyi ma nas phyis du mchi ba'i rngo lta ga la thogs te/de ni gnas ma mchis so//de bzhin du glang po che chen po lta bu 'di dag gi bdud du bdug ni dmus long dang mtshungs par mthor 
8b6(gzungs): ste/sangs rgyas kyi chos 'di dag ni de tsam du rgya che ba lags so//bdag ni sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams kyi snod du ma gyur te/da ci bgyid yang ma mchis so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/sha ra dwa ti'i bu de skad ma zer cig/sha 
8b7(gzungs): ra dwa ti'i bu 'di lnga ste/dper na de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'od kyis sems can ji snyed cig la reg pa de dag thams cad sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams thob pa yin no//sha ra dwa ti'i bu de bzhin du khyod ni ting nge 'dzin mtha' yas shing bsam gyis mi khyab pa thob pa yin no//
9a1(gzungs): @#//bcom ldan 'das kyis chos kyi rnam grangs 'di bshad pa na/lha dang mi'i skyed bu las sems can dgu khri nyis stong gis chos mngon par rtogs par gyur to//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi blo gros byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin 
9a2(gzungs): dkon mchog ta la la sbyor ba mtha' yas pa zhes bya ba de la snyoms par zhugs so//byang chub sems dpa' chos gyi blo gros snyoms par zhugs ma thag tu phyogs bcu'i phyogs re re'i 'jig rten gyi khams sangs rgyas kyi zhing stong phrag bcu'i rdul phra rab kyi rdul snyed kyi 
9a3(gzungs): pha rol nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das sangs rgyas kyi zhing stong prag bcu'i rdul phra rab kyi rngul snyed kyis zhal mngon par bstan te/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/de dag thams cad kyang mtshan rdo rje'i snying po zhes bya bar mthun pa sha stag go//phyogs gcig ji lta bar

9a5(gzungs): rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad 'di nyid kyi sngon gyi smon lam dang /sngon gyi byin gyi rlabs dang /khyod nyid kyi dge ba'i rtsa bas nye bar brtan pas 'di lta ste chos thams cad bstan par bya ba dang /sangs rgyas 
9a6(gzungs): kyi ye shes rnam par sbyang ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi ye shes rnam par 'phel bar bya ba dang /chos kyi dbyings su 'jug par bya ba dang /sems can gyi khams rnam par dgrol ba dang /chags pa med pa'i ye shes la 'jug cing chung bar bya ba dang /kun du sbyor bar 
9a7(gzungs): bya ba dang /skad thams cad brjod par bya ba dang /thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes la 'jug par bya ba dang /chos thams cad la sgrib pa med par bya ba dang /dus gsum thams cad yongs su shes pa'i chos bstan pa la 'jug par bya ba'i phyir phyogs bcu 
9b1(gzungs): nas de bzhin gshegs pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing stong phrag bcu'i rdul phra rab kyi rdul snyed thams cad kyang mtshan mthun par 'di dag thams cad khyod la byin gyis rlob kyis rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/byang chub sems dpa' rnam par gzhag pa bcu las brtsams te/sangs

9b3(gzungs): dang|brjed pa med pa'i chos nyid dang /tha dad pa med pa'i nge shes dang /kha na ma tho ba med pa dang /zil gyis mi non pa dang /zhum pa med pa dang /'grogs pa med pa'i chos nyid dang/brjod pa bzang po nye bar sgrub pa mdzad do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar 
9b4(gzungs): ting nge 'dzin 'di nyid kyi chos nyid thob pa'i phyir ro//de nas sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das de dag gis phyag g.yas pa brkyang ste/byang chub sems dpa'achos kyi blo gros kyi mgo la nyug pa mdzad de/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasade dag gis byang chub sems
9b5(gzungs): chos kyi blo gros la reg ma thag tu/de nas ting nge 'dzin de las langs nas byang chub sems dpa'ade dag la smras pa/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi rigs 'di ni 'di lta ste/chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa dang /nam mkha'i khams kyi   
9b6(gzungs): mthas gtugs pas rgya che ba'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams ni 'das pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi rigs su skye ba yin/ma byon pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi rigs suskye

41b3(gzungs): med cing chos med de//dgon par 'gro dang thar pa mi dmigs pa//der ni rgyal srid 'byor pa rab spangs te//zhi ba'i yid kyis gnas med mngon par 'byung //de dag khyim dang 'ching dang srid pa'i gnas//'gro ba thams cad yongs su dgrol ba'i 
41b4(gzungs): phyir//'dod pa'i dga' ba kun la mi gnas par//mngon par byung zhing thar pa rab tu brjod//de dag spyod pa bcu rnams rjod byed cing //skyes chen rnams kyi chos spyod gang yin dang //drang srong rnams kyi spyod kun ma lus par//yongs su brjod cing 'gro

97a6(gzungs): mchog rnams kyis rab tu gsungs//de gzigs dge ba dus brnyes//sangs rgyas blo gros rgyan gyis bzhugs//sems dpa' blo gros che//bskal pa gcig tu spyod pas kyang //brgyud mar sangs rgyas kun 'byung zhing //spyod pa du ma 'byung ba yi//yon tan brgya 
97a7(gzungs): yi spos ldan 'di//thos pa spro bar mi nus so//longs spyod rgya mtsho chen po can//byang chub sems dpa' mchog gi dbang po ru//rgyal mchog rnams kyis dbang bskur ba'i//phyir mi ldog pa'i chos can ldan//'di thos sangs rgyas spyod pa yi//sems can kun 
97b1(gzungs): kyi sdig pa rnams//ma lus rab tu bsal nas ni//blo chen dri ma med par 'gyur//zhi zhing dag byed bkra shis la//rdul med brgyan te zhi bar byed//dge zhing mya ngan thams cad sel//sdug bsngal thams cad nges par 'joms//dbang byed gsang sngags dam pa 
97b2(gzungs): ste//sa stengs sdig pa thams cad sel//ma smad 'bras bu nges par sbyin//bstan pa yin te sdang ba 'dul//nyes pa byas pa sel ba yin//gzungs 'di gang gis thos gyur de//sangs rgyas rnyed par dka' ba yang //ring por mi thogs thob par 'gyur//
97b3(gzungs): sgrib pa thams cad rab zhi zhing //khyab bdag nyon mongs kun sel ba//gang zhig nyon mongs zhi byed pa'i//gzungs 'di 'chang bar byed pa yis//kun du bzang po nyid dang ni//las na rdo rje nyid kyang 'thob//rig sngags 'chang gi bdag po yang //'di thos pas ni 
97b4(gzungs): thob par 'gyur//'jig rten kun na lam mo ba//bcom ldan rnam par snang mdzad kyang //dkyil 'khor dam pa gting med pa//'di ni ji srid ma gsan par//rab zhi nyon mongs 'dul ba yi//rang 'byung go 'phang ma brnyes te//rnam par dag phyir gsan 
97b5(gzungs): gyur nas//gsan ma thag tu rnam par dag/de phyir phun sum tshogs pa dang //legs par 'dod pas brjod par bya//gzhan gyis mi thub gzungs gsan nas//thub pa kun gyis gang brnyes pa//'khor ba 'dir ni nam du yang //bdud kyi las dang 'khrug med 
97b6(gzungs): dang //mya ngan med cer 'thab med pa//gzungs la gnas pas thob par 'gyur//gnas kyi dam pa thob nas kyang //thub pa dri ma med pa'i blo//gzigs pa bzang rnams gar gshegs pa'i//bzang 'gro der ni 'gro bar 'gyur//legs pa 'dod pa 
97b7(gzungs): rnams kyis ni//tshig gi mdo 'di mnyan par gyis//nyon mongs zad par bya ba la//rgyal bas gsungs pa'i gzungs 'di mchog/sdig pa byed cing dge la sdang //sdig pa'i rang bzhin rab 'jungs pa//gang gis thos nas de spong ba//nga yis mngon sum 'byor bar 
98a1(gzungs): @#//'gyur//sdig pa gzhan yang mi bya zhing //snga ma rnams kyang bshags par bya//gzungs mchog 'di yi mthu stobs kyis//sdig pa thams cad 'byang bar 'gyur//tshe rabs gzhan ni stong dag dang //tshe rabs bye ba phrag brgyar yang //sdig 
98a2(gzungs): pa mi dge gang byas pa//dag 'gyur 'di la the tshom med//yar gyi ngo yi tshes brgyad dang //de bzhin tshes ni bco lnga la//bsnyen gnas legs par lhur blangs la//cho ga bzhin du sangs rgyas mchod//de nas rmongs pa med par yang //nyon mongs dgra ni rab 
98a3(gzungs): 'joms shing //shin tu thub dka' dpe med pa//nyes bzlog pa'i gzungs brjod na//thub pa'i zhal gyi zla ba'i 'od//'bar ba nges par mthong bar 'gyur//des na tshogs rnams rdzogs pa yi//rgyal ba'i sras po nyid du 'gyur//'od dpag med la mnga' ba yi//

98b1(gzungs): dri ma sel//dri ma med pa skyon bral ba//thub pa sangs rgyas shin tu zhi//'jig rten mgon po skye dgu'i bdag/de bzhin gshegs pa nyon mongs thul//de bzhin rgyal ba rdul bsal ba//ston pa ston mdzad brtan pa ste//sdig pa med pa rdul dang bral//

98b7(gzungs): spras pa'i bdag nyid ya mtshan ston//dka' ba med pa'i ston pa po//de bzhin nges pa bde sbyin pa//mthu can yangs pa rgya che ba//bdud bzhi'i stobs rnams sel mdzad pa//brtson chen spro ba che ba ste//dgyes pa chen po stobs po che//mthu brnyes

99b1(gzungs): bar 'char ba brnyes//rigs chen mthun par skyes pa ste//de bzhin gshegs pa'i dge ba'i gnas//khyad par brnyes pa mnyam nyid brnyes//mi mnyam sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam//don chen shin tu mthar phyin pa//nyon mongs bral ba thub par dka'//

99b4(gzungs): gsung ba chen po ye shes che//gnas chen bkra shis chen po ste//mchod par gyur pa yul las 'das//bya ba'i mtshan nyid med pa po//sangs rgyas yul du skyes pa ste//shes bya'i spyod yul rgya che ba//kun mkhyen longs spyod 'bras bu can//
99b5(gzungs): 'jig rten dbang 'das spyod yul ba//kun mkhyen yul gyi snang ba ste//thabs mnga' phun sum tshogs ldan pa//rdzu 'phrul rol pa'i thabs dang ldan//chos kyi gar thabs ston pa po//rab ston yon tan sgyur mdzad pa//spyod pa ma lus 'dul bar mdzad//
99b6(gzungs): yongs su rdzogs pa rnam par dag/sangs rgyas spyod yul rnam sangs rgyas//bgegs byed ma legs byed pa bcom//bdud kyi dregs pa sel bar mdzad//g.yul ngor rnam par rgyal ba brnyes//dpa' bo chen po pham med stobs//theg pa chen po'i 
99b7(gzungs): shing rta bcibs//blo chen rgyal bar dka' ba yi//nyon mongs rgya mtsho'i pha rol gshegs//theg pa chen po'i tshul la gnas//theg pa mchog la longs spyod pa//nyan thos 'das pa'i spyod yul can//rang rgyal las kyang 'das pa ste//sangs rgyas 
100a1(gzungs): @#//sa yi 'bras bur gnas//gong nas gong du khyad par mkhyen//khyod ni nam mkha'i gnas na bzhugs//bde 'byung sa dang zhing rnams kyi//mtsho chen dag la longs par spyod//sangs rgyas dbang po rgyas pa la//mtshan brgya rtsa brgyad kyis

102a1(gzungs): @#//rgyas stong phrag mang pos yongs bstod pa//bskal pa rab 'byam mang por mkhas pa ste//dkyil 'khor mang po dag na dpa' ba'i sku//'jig rten 'das pa'i mthu mnga' ston pa po//rgya chen dri med dkyil 'khor dbus na bzhugs//rab

102a4(gzungs): 'khor ba//pad+ma bzang po'i rigs mchog thig les mtshan//spyan bzang dge ba'i kun nas rten gyur pa//gsang sngags mang po 'dzin pa'i dbang phyug lo//tshogs chen mdun ma 'dus pa'i mnga' bdag lags//spyan gsum dag gis dkyil 'khor 
102a5(gzungs): mdzes gyur pa//ka la ping ka'i skad kyis sdang ba 'dul//mnga' dang ru gzhu stobs chen mtshon cha bsnams//stobs kyi lha ste stobs dang brtson pas 'dul//rgyan rnams sna tshogs legs par sbas pa ste//rgyal ba rig sngags 'chang ba mang pos bstod//
